# Sync sessions — 2026-11

Rows moved verbatim from `docs/SYNC_STATUS.md` (2026-11-08, DOC1, the frontmatter series documented once): the landed
frontmatter rows of its "Silent-loss inventory" — the batch rows 77d-108b, the observer's families (S2b), the Z rows
Z1-Z14, the bundle rows int117a-int145b, the censuses and measurements that retired. A row whose work landed with a live
residual left a one-line row in SYNC_STATUS holding the residual (its witnesses, REDs, OD numbers) and pointing here.
Also the fixed frontmatter rows of `perfect_kernel/OPEN_ROWS.md` (its fidelity audit round 2 table and one RED-repro
item), each under where it was; `parity/DEFERRED_FAMILIES.md` held none fixed. The live plan is
[`../parity/FRONTMATTER_SINGLE_ENGINE_PLAN.md`](../parity/FRONTMATTER_SINGLE_ENGINE_PLAN.md).

| — | **Frontmatter architecture: the audit's plan** (`archive/FRONTMATTER_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md`; the user's principle 2026-10-28: bindings only through the Frontmatter API, general reorganizing abstractions at every stage, special cases local in bindings or behind API opt-in data): the pipeline is Perl's (queue → digest → place) and the Rust-only API extensions are opt-in data (`owner=sequence`, `_share`, `\lx@title@inst`); ranked work — (1) the observer policy table keyed on binding file names in generic code (`observer/policy.rs:98-260`, `written: true` for revtex/ams) becomes binding-declared conventions; (2) one kill switch per general reorganizer (86g cover order, #262 placement, #246 wrap, `distribute_upfront_contacts`, `respell_emails`, K11 reroute, deposit keep), defaults unchanged, for exact A/B attribution; (3) an author-convention registry merging the ~7 drifted vocabulary lists in `author_block/` (llncs `\inst`/`\fnmsep`, ACM `\alignauthor`, IJCAI `\affiliations`, IEEE `\IEEEauthorrefmark`, `\thanksref`, `\altaffilmark`), bindings declaring their entries; (4) 86g: a sentence-length `\noindent` paragraph is not cover unless deposit-stamped (about 39 of the 295 covers rest on `ltx_noindent` alone; a CONFIRMED body-prose probe in the audit); (5) contrib bindings building `<ltx:classification>`/`<ltx:abstract>`/contacts in place (siamart, interact, jmlr, jmlr2e, ieeeojcsys, ieeetaes, agujournal2019, optica_article, dmtcs_episciences; svmult `\lx@sv@chapauthor`) go through the API; (6) the IJCAI sectioned split becomes a generic `\lx@add@authors@sectioned`; (7) a shared `frontmatter::tokens` toolkit for the six contrib bindings that re-implement the reader's primitives (ptephy, osa_journal, epl, eptcs, aistats, interspeech), as each is touched; (8) record `distribute_upfront_contacts`' grid as charter §4's list evidence or gate it; (9) one ownership rule with one home — DECIDED (user 2026-10-28: expand the home that started as the general layer): the observer (OD #837, FM S2/K23, "a single resolver" over what TeX prints) is the single home; no new ownership rule goes into `author_block/`, `shared_row` or the insert passes; the old reader keeps what feeds the events and retires per the S2 inventory; 85a's and 85b's rules are ported into the resolver when `article` and llncs reach S2c; item (1) — binding-declared conventions instead of the name-keyed policy table — comes first; **item (1) DONE (87a)**; item (7) WITHDRAWN 2026-10-29 by the digested-representation refactor (next row): each binding declares its conventions at load (`declare_frontmatter_conventions`, its `FRONTMATTER_CONVENTIONS`), the observer reads them from the state, `KERNEL` for a family that declares none; the table is gone (OD #837 "The conventions' home"; a pure refactor: census records and A/B unchanged; guard `perfect_kernel_batch87a::*`)  **Item 9 DECIDED by the user 2026-11-01 and made the frontmatter priority:** "keep evolving our design of the Frontmatter API until it can accommodate all variations used in arXiv. Prioritize refactoring to a better model of a single ownership engine, unified machinery and API, unified placement order inside the API" — one ownership engine (reader + insert-time passes and the observer converge), every binding's placement through the shared machinery (no binding-private splits: llncs, sv_support, inst_support, aa, ptephy, webofc), one placement order inside the API (marks/labels → the name a contact spells → the class's declared convention for unmarked lines → the block), bindings declaring conventions only (the ~95 per-binding `annotate=` modes: run 42, new 35, all 16, last 1, N 1). Failures of the old design that motivated it: rev97cd C1 (sv_support's split put an institute's e-mail on every author: 2101.04527, 2206.03859, 2404.15833) and F1 (aastex `annotate=run` overriding marks: 1810.01941 39 → 364 affiliations). Design study done: the plan is `parity/FRONTMATTER_SINGLE_ENGINE_PLAN.md` (the observer emits owners at the flush as Perl's own `label=`/`annotations=` keys instead of patching the old reader's DOM; Stages 0-7; rulings R1-R9); its stages are the batches after int126a. | OPEN (plan) |

| — | **Frontmatter notes read as the class prints them; a note with no ink is none (92e, 92g; user rulings 2026-10-30, FIDELITY_CHARTER §2, §4)** (OD #915, #916, #921): fixed by `name=` where the class prints another label — aa/svjour `\offprints` "Send offprint requests to:", jheppub `\arxivnumber` "ArXiv ePrint:", acmart `\acmISBN` "ACM ISBN", iopart `\submitto`/`\submitted` "Submitted to:", AASTeX `\submitjournal` "Submitted to", IEEEoj/ieeetj `\doiinfo` and old LIPIcs `\DOI` "Digital Object Identifier"; a note is dropped only when it provably prints nothing — every item known blank (`leaves_no_ink`: white space, glue, kerns, breaks, struts, phantoms, an unframed box or a constructor whose literal template prints only its arguments, over blank arguments) —, emptied by us (`\offprints{\email{…}}`: astro-ph0405280, astro-ph0608669) or by the author (`\thanks{}`, `\externaleditor{~}`: 2004.01582, 2401.15379, 2609.00671); any doubt is ink (a constructor's own text, `\openone`, `\dots`, `\LaTeX`, `$\vdots$`; an element of its own; a closure or a construction hook, `\emph{}`, `\url{}`; a sized rule, a frame, a leader, an error marker; a background colour, `\colorbox{red}{}`), and the observer drops the same notes on the same input (`ruling4:inkless`; a note's text is no part of the block's empty-mark filter). | — | **106t: the title-page check's second source — what TeX makes of what the document gave its frontmatter commands (Bundle 4; OD #953 "106t")** — DONE (r2, rule (A): digest, never read tokens): a check, the XML the base's. A class binding that writes its own frontmatter has no print run, and a binding's command may drop what pdflatex prints. The calls of LaTeXML's macros read from the document's own text (`Expandable::watched_call` → `state::note_document_call`, each argument with whether the body names it) inside which the frontmatter API queues an entry (`title_page_check::note_frontmatter_entry`) are its frontmatter commands; wanted beside the print, all digested: the entry's own digested content (`end_frontmatter_entry_digestion`, `ink_word_text`), and, digested once speculatively (sect05.rs `speculative_word_run`), a required argument the command's body never names (quantumarticle `\ead`, KPE #1208), an internal command's optional one (`\begin{abstract}[Summary]`, KPE #1209) and the document's text a binding-made macro drops while the entry digests (`state::note_dropped_argument`, filtered to what the document wrote: IEEEtran's `\lx@IEEE@membership@drop`, jmlr's `\titletag`). A binding's by-design drop is declared on its own definition, `DefMacro!(…, drops => [n])` (`Expandable::dropped_by_design`, the expansion unchanged), only where the real file prints nothing of the argument or prints it as page furniture: hyperref `\texorpdfstring` #2 (2306.06741, 2306.06006), dmtcs `\publicationdetails` #5, lipics `\volumeinfo`, lmcs `\lmcsheading`, aomart `\subject` #2, midl `\orcid`, cas `\corref`, icml/OmniBus `\comment` (main-session ruling (b)). r1's 42 token-reading false alarms pass unchanged; two guards re-pinned to true losses pdflatex prints (`batch86i` amsart `[Résumé]`, `batch86d` the pinned residual's IEEE grade). **Coverage** (98 lost-words papers): 24 → 28 (26 all, 2 some: 2209.07123, 2104.14704, 1511.03982, 2211.07413); r1's token reading named 4 more that digestion cannot (2110.09105 aa `\email jose…` an undelimited argument; 1107.2462, 2101.10506, 2203.00070 raw classes with no kept print run — 106s's reach). **False alarms:** 0 over a 500-paper bound-class control from `popint125a_s10k` and 500 random. (1) `NameKeys::of`, the one place every spelling site reads, keys a name by its base letters and by `resolver::transliteration` (ä ae, ö oe, ü ue, ß ss, å aa, ø oe, æ ae, œ oe), an accent or letter command read as the letter it prints (`spelling_name_text`); callers pass the name unfolded; `respell_emails` moves an e-mail by the name as printed or transliterated, never base-folded (`NameKeys::as_printed`; user ruling 2026-11-05: `joerg.mueller@` is Jörg Müller's, so charter §4 #420's `mueller-group@` example moves to him, `muller-group@` stays); a word's length class is its base spelling's, a literal-vs-transliterated tie stays a tie (`mueller@` beside Hans Mueller and Jörg Müller: the block; user ruling 2026-11-05), and a surname's six-letter prefix never reuses the given name's letters (review rev107a; the last also fixes a pre-existing `andrea.rossi@` on Andrea Andreassen). | — | **108b: the silent lost words counted — the title-page check's third source (brief 108b; OD #953 "108b")** — FIXED (a check; one XML change, item C). What the document's text gives a raw class's or own-directory file's setter before the title page is digested and wanted: `Warning:frontmatter:unclaimed_store` for a word only it wants, an Info `frontmatter:unread_store` for a store no code reads but page furniture, a `\csname` store, an optional argument, or digits from an argument with no counted word. A document end in such an argument is inert in every speculative run (`gullet::speculative_digestion`; 106t's run lost the body before it too). Bounds said in Infos. 5 of the 7 silent dev3 papers now counted (2305.00867, 1011.2770, 2408.05088, 2605.02209, 1401.7920); B, a binding's drop in the document's macro (2503.14354); C, `\@listi`'s size10.clo bodies (2310.07393: 2 Errors → 0, "ampus" → "Campus"). On 2,530 papers: raw-class 14.3 / 1,000 warned, 9.0 false (read against the whole PDF, mostly stores under a switch that is off: vgtc's `\papertype`, `\category`); others 5.7 / 1,000, 3.6 false. CMS papers +3.2 to +3.9 % `instructions:u`, the check's whole-document scan an unheld word starts. ### From "Perfect-kernel RED repros without a row elsewhere"

- **FIXED int26 (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #754, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #980) — a `{abstract}` left open at `\end{document}` empties the document** (SHARED; 65c review side finding): the deferred
  abstract body (`\lx@add@frontmatter@until … \lx@end@abstract`) runs to the end, holding the finalize whatsit and the
  rest of the document, and is never placed — `<document/>` with or without a following `\section` (Perl: resources
  only, 1 error); pdflatex prints every word with 1 error. RED `sectioning-frontmatter/unclosed_abstract_at_end_document_keeps_the_document`.
